### Pitfalls (learned the hard way today)
- **Never `kill -ABRT` SpringBoard**: the jailbreak enters safe mode (tweaks off). Crashing **wifid** is safe, but **restarting wifid while the UI is up has three times been followed by the USB network (ue0, 192.168.7.x) and the console log dying** (phone alive, ue0 UP on the phone, PC cannot reach it; a reboot restores it) and once by a ~3 minute Wi-Fi stall. Cause unknown. Reboot instead of restarting wifid; boot-time start is fine.
- When USB is gone use Wi-Fi: `ssh -o UserKnownHostsFile=/dev/null -o StrictHostKeyChecking=no root@192.168.1.14` (no password/key setup needed); reboot with `ssh ... '/var/jb/sbin/reboot'` then, in fastboot: `fastboot -s $SWEET_SERIAL set_active b && fastboot -s $SWEET_SERIAL boot IMAGE` (only temporary boots, never flash; never `fastboot getvar`).
- The firmware's rate control can get stuck at a low rate (throughput 0.3 Mbit/s, RTT 150+ ms). Fix without reboot: `sysctl -w kern.sweet_wlan_vparam=$((0x1b000004)); sleep 2; sysctl -w kern.sweet_wlan_vparam=$((0x1b000000))` (fixed OFDM 24, then auto). Cause unknown; a watchdog using peer stats would be the proper fix.
- The phone has no `pkill`/`awk`/`strings`/`curl`/`nc`; use `ps ax | grep "[x]"` + `kill`, `sweet-bw` (throughput, server: `python3 tools/sweet-bw-server.py` on the PC, PC LAN address 192.168.1.22), `sweet-a80211-probe [scan]` (calls Apple's library), `sweet-shot 1 /tmp/shot.bin` + `python3 tools/sweet-shot.py` for screenshots (transfer the file in a separate ssh command or the dump is corrupted).
- Shared tree/phone with the other Claude session (`debug-usb-ethernet-ping`): tell it before rebooting or building; commit with explicit pathspec, unsigned: `git -c commit.gpgsign=false commit -m ... -- <paths>`, ending with the Co-Authored-By / Claude-Session lines. Standing constraints: never write modemst1/modemst2/fsg/persist, keep dumps/firmware/credentials out of git (research/, logs/, shim/out are git-ignored), keep the GPU boot-args and the plain 93,748-byte `shim/out/sunfish.adt` (never regenerate it with mkadt.py). The hotspot password the user gave is not stored in git.
- On the phone (not in git): `/System/Library/xpc/launchd.plist` (wifid enabled; original `/var/root/launchd.plist.orig`, also in research/wifi/), `/var/root/sweet-wlan-route.sh` (= tools/sweet-wlan-route.sh), `/var/jb/usr/bin/{sweet-ifnamer,sweet-autoconf6,sweet-bw,sweet-a80211-probe,sweet-wifi,sweet-wlan}`, `/fw/wlan/net.conf` (SSID + PMK of the network to join), the other session's `/var/jb/.procursus_strapped` marker.
- Reverse-engineering material (git-ignored, regenerate with `ipsw dyld extract ~/decomp/dyld/dyld_shared_cache_arm64 IO80211 MobileWiFi SystemConfiguration -o research/wifi/dylibs`): `research/wifi/apple80211_ioc_table.txt` (418 request types), `research/wifi/apple80211_event_table.txt` (event codes: 1 power, 2 ssid, 3 bssid, 4 link, 9 assoc done, 10 scan done, 15 scan cache updated, 39 rssi changed ...), crash-report stacks `research/wifi/{springboard-hang,wifid-hang,wifid-spinner}.ips`. Disassemble with `ipsw dyld disass ~/decomp/dyld/dyld_shared_cache_arm64 --symbol <name>` (e.g. `__eventRead`, `_Apple80211Scan`, `__addScanResultToList`, `__dispatchAsyncAssocEvent`).

## 2026-10-28 ~23:30 Wi-Fi milestone: Apple80211 raw SSID, AUTH_TYPE, PROTMODE, ASSOCIATE_RESULT, and stateless scan cursor (Patch 0171)
- **Patch 0171** (`xnu/patches/0171-sweet-wlan-apple80211-authtype-assoc-result.patch`, commit `35c0b11`):
  - **SSID buffer**: IO80211 `_Apple80211GetWithIOCTL` allocates a `CFMutableData` and copies `rq->req_len` bytes directly from `rq->req_data`. The previous `{version, len, ssid}` struct header corrupted CFString with control characters. Switched to writing raw SSID bytes and `rq->req_len = slen`. `sweet-a80211-probe` now confirms clean `"SSID_STR" = "<HOME-WIFI>"` matching the known-networks cache.
  - **AUTH_TYPE (2)**: returns 16 bytes `{version=1, auth_lower=1 (Open System), auth_upper=8 (WPA2-PSK), auth_upper_ext=0}`.
  - **PROTMODE (6)**: returns 12 bytes `{version=1, mode=0, threshold=0}`.
  - **req_val handlers**: POWERSAVE (5, 0), INT_MIT (18, 0), ASSOCIATE_RESULT (21, 0 = success), TX_POWER (26, 100 mW), POWER_SAVE_LEVEL (57, 0), NSS (353, 1), COUNTRY_BAND_SUPPORT (423, 3 = 2.4G | 5G).
  - **Stateless SCAN_RESULT (11)**: IO80211 iterates scan records using `rq->req_val` as the index, incrementing by 1 until the driver returns `EIO`. Replacing the global `g_a80211_cursor` with `rq->req_val` indexing makes scans stateless per request, eliminating concurrent scan collisions between `wifid` and `Preferences`.
  - **ASSOC_DONE event (9)**: populated 16-byte payload with 6-byte BSSID at offset 0 and status 0 at offset 12 (`__dispatchAsyncAssocEvent`).
  - **RSSI_BOUNDS (106)**, **CHANNELS_INFO (207)**, **COLOCATED_NETWORK_SCOPE_ID (387)** implemented.
- **Hardware verification**: Booted `shim/out/shim-local-0191-authtype.img` (kernel-0171-authtype). Device reached ping at 6s; SSH up over USB (`192.168.7.2`) and Wi-Fi (`192.168.1.14`). `sweet-a80211-probe` confirmed all status fields and dumped all 18 visible BSS networks without drops.
- **User-reported UI / display quirks noted**:
  - Blurred UI elements color flash: user reports blurred UI elements sometimes flash weird colors like green, specifically the "x" app delete badge when holding wallpaper / jiggle mode and the lockscreen clock when viewing notifications (especially on dots wallpaper). Parked for graphics/Mesa session; do not fix now.
  - Phantom key / volume slider HUD / auto-lock: user reports phone randomly turns on, volume slider HUD appears periodically, and auto-lock doesn't turn screen off after 30 seconds. Confirmed: SPMI key noise in `sweet_keys.c` wakes screen / resets `IOPMrootDomain` idle timer, and missing CommCenter causes `__SBTelephonyDaemonRestartHandler` to reset idle timer every 1s. Parked for userland tweak session.
- **Next steps**:
  1. Dynamic rescan on `SCAN_REQ` (run `wp_scan()` in background worker thread and post `SCAN_DONE` event 10 upon completion so pull-to-refresh works in Settings).
  2. Association from Settings UI (`ASSOCIATE` 20 / `DISASSOCIATE` 22): parse PSK/PMK in `apple80211_assoc_req` from `wifid` and trigger `jn_join()`.

## 2026-10-29 ~01:05 Wi-Fi milestone: SETTINGS.APP BLUE CHECKMARK WORKING (Spinner Solved, en0, IO80211Controller Hierarchy)
- **Settings Wi-Fi Spinner Completely Solved (Milestone 0173)**:
  - Disassembled `WiFiKit` (`-[WFNetworkListController _updateCurrentNetworkIPState]` at `0x1f57700c8`): checks `[ipMonitor hasValidIPAddress]`. If `NO` -> state 1 (SPINNER / JOINING). If `YES` -> state 2 (BLUE CONNECTED CHECKMARK).
  - Reverse engineering `-[WFIPMonitor __wifiServiceID]` revealed two critical Apple constraints:
    1. **Hardcoded Substr "en" Requirement**: `0x1f57b09fc: [bsdName rangeOfString:@"en"]`. If BSD name does not contain `"en"`, `WiFiKit` logs `"%s: skipping non-Infra WiFi service bsdName='%@'"` and rejects the interface as peer-to-peer! The previous kernel name `"ew0"` caused instant rejection. Renamed to `"en0"`.
    2. **IO80211Controller Parent Requirement**: SystemConfiguration `_processNetworkInterface` (`0x19064a750`) checks `IOObjectConformsTo(parent, "IO80211Controller")` for interface type 6 (Ethernet). When matched, SC marks interface type as `"IEEE80211"`, satisfying `CFEqual(type, kSCNetworkInterfaceTypeIEEE80211)`.
  - In `tools/xnu-docker/work/xnu/iokit/Kernel/arm/SweetWiFi.cpp`: defined `IO80211Controller`, `IONetworkInterface`, `IOEthernetInterface`, and `IO80211Interface`. Allocates controller, registers service, attaches `IO80211Interface` with prefix `"en"`.
  - In `tools/xnu-docker/work/xnu/bsd/dev/sweet_mpss.c`: changed interface creation to `init.name = "en"` (`en0`), Apple80211 event interface name to `"en0"`, and ioctl calls to `en0`.
  - In `scratch/gen_sc_plists.py`: generated `NetworkInterfaces.plist` (`en0`, `SCNetworkInterfaceType = IEEE80211`) and `preferences.plist` (`serviceID = 4CD8DB21-0AC8-40A5-83C6-094D249CFE68`, `DeviceName = en0`, `Hardware = AirPort`, `Type = Ethernet`). Deployed to `/private/var/preferences/SystemConfiguration/`.
  - In `userland/net/sweet-wlan.c`: updated `#define SERVICE "4CD8DB21-0AC8-40A5-83C6-094D249CFE68"`, `#define IFNAME "en0"`, published IPv4/DNS under both service UUID and interface `en0`, published `State:/Network/Global/IPv4`, cleared `IPConfigurationBusy`.
  - In `tools/sweet-wlan-route.sh`: updated all `ew0` to `en0`.
  - In `userland/net/sweet-a80211-probe.c`: updated bind to `en0`.
- **Hardware Verification (on `shim/out/shim-local-0193-en0.img`)**:
  - `test_sc` confirms:
    `SCNetworkInterfaceCopyAll`: found `[0] bsd=en0, type=IEEE80211, name=Wi-Fi`.
    `SCPreferences`: `serviceID=4CD8DB21-0AC8-40A5-83C6-094D249CFE68 -> bsd=en0, type=IEEE80211`.
  - `sweet-wlan` published `4CD8DB21-0AC8-40A5-83C6-094D249CFE68`.
  - Opened Settings > Wi-Fi: **SPINNER IS GONE. Connected network (`<HOME-WIFI>`) shows the BLUE CONNECTED CHECKMARK, Lock icon, 3-bar signal icon, and blue (i) info button!**
  - Captured on-device screenshot: `logs/shot-check.png` (and `logs/shot-check.bin`).
  - Internet latency over Wi-Fi (`en0`): IPv4 ping 1.1.1.1 = 5.5 ms (0% loss), IPv6 ping6 2001:4860:4860::8888 = 6.0 ms (0% loss).

## 2026-10-29 Wi-Fi: real rescan on SCAN_REQ, patch 0173 (Claude)
- Patches 0170-0171 (Antigravity) and 0172 (en0 + IO80211Controller parent; I wrote the patch file from the source tree, commit 4cbf1db) are in. Settings > Wi-Fi shows the blue checkmark for the connected network (Antigravity, 0173 milestone above).

Otherwise the cache answers at once and the event is posted immediately.

Most neighbours are at -85..-95 dBm and wifid's `WiFiScanResultsPreparer` ("prominent filtering") hides weak ones; not verified that this is the reason.

A weak network is not heard in every 3 s passive scan, so entries are now dropped only after three scans in a row without it (`wp.bss[].missed`); image `shim-local-0195-age.img` (kernel-0180-age). ## 2026-10-29 Wi-Fi: why Settings shows only a few networks (patch 0175, Claude)
- Observed (user + wifid log): Settings > Wi-Fi shows the connected network with the blue checkmark but 0-2 other networks that come and go, although wifid ingests 25-31 networks from every scan. The scan request Settings sends contains `minRSSI=-80` (see "ScanParams" in `oslog --info --debug -p wifid`), and WiFiKit has `applyRssiThresholdFilter` / `rssiThreshold` (WFScanRequest, set in `-[WFScanManager _processNextScan]`): iOS hides every network weaker than -80 dBm. Most neighbours are at -81..-95 dBm (our own network at -55..-64), the ones around -80 flicker.
- Not the cause: `GET KNOWN NETWORKS LIST err=2` still appears with an empty `/private/var/preferences/com.apple.wifi.known-networks.plist` (created, harmless, binary plist `{}`); `.. network records count: N` doubles (62 for 31) because two SCAN requests per cycle each post SCAN_DONE and the async scan accumulates both batches (harmless, wifid dedups).
- Patch 0175: `kern.sweet_wlan_rssi_offset` (default +10 dB) is added to the signal strength of scanned networks in Apple80211 scan results (not to the connected network's), so networks down to about -90 dBm pass Settings' filter. Image `shim-local-0197-rssioff.img` (kernel-0181-rssioff; includes the other session's 0174 NativeAdrenoGpuPublished). Result not yet reported by the user.
- Also: aging of scan results (0173, three misses in a row) and the rescan itself are in; the on-request scan takes ~3 s and briefly stalls traffic (RTT spikes up to 2 s were seen while Settings kept scanning every ~20 s).

- An overnight soak monitor writes `logs/soak-overnight.txt` (git-ignored): one health line per minute (uptime, router ping, last `EW:` stats line, number of trouble lines in the event log) and a TCP throughput line every 10 minutes (needs `python3 tools/sweet-bw-server.py` on the PC, port 5201/5202). If the phone is rebooted by the other session the lines show a small uptime again.
- Morning test list for the user: Settings > Wi-Fi: do more networks stay on the list with the +10 dB offset (`sysctl kern.sweet_wlan_rssi_offset`, adjustable); then tap another network (e.g. the hotspot "<HOTSPOT>", WPA2/WPA3 mixed): the kernel does not implement ASSOCIATE yet and refuses with 102, but logs the first three `A80211 SET 20 ...` requests as hex (`sysctl -n kern.sweet_mpss_events | grep "SET 20"`): that dump is what is needed to implement joining (SSID, BSSID, key/PMK layout in `apple80211_assoc_data`). A direct call of `Apple80211Associate(handle, scanResultDict, password)` from `sweet-a80211-probe assoc SSID PW` returns -3903 without any ioctl, so that guessed signature is wrong; do not spend time on it.
- Disassembly notes for `_performAssociation` (research/wifi/dylibs/IO80211, via `ipsw dyld disass ... --symbol __performAssociation`): it reads the dictionary keys SSID, BSSID, FORCE_BSSID, AP_MODE, ASSOC_FLAGS, ASSOC_REASON, BAND_PREFERENCE, WAPI, FT_STATE, COLOCATED_NETWORK_SCOPE_ID, PRIVATE_MAC_ADDRESS*, calls `Apple80211ParseWPAPassword` for the password, then `Apple80211RawSet` (the association ioctl) and `Apple80211SetWithIOCTL(h, 0x16 = DISASSOCIATE, ...)` before/after, and `__waitForJoinResult` (waits for event ASSOC_DONE 9 with {status, reason}; the kernel must post it when the join finishes).
